Report Matrix!A1	INTEGRATED CONTRIBUTION SETTLEMENT RETURN (PILA) - SOCIAL SECURITY AND PAYROLL LEVIES — REPORT MATRIX
Report Matrix!A3	Report ID
Report Matrix!B3	CO-PILA-003
Report Matrix!A4	Report Name (EN)
Report Matrix!B4	Integrated Contribution Settlement Return (PILA) - social security and payroll levies
Report Matrix!A5	Report Name (Local)
Report Matrix!B5	Planilla Integrada de Liquidacion de Aportes (PILA)
Report Matrix!A6	Document ID
Report Matrix!B6	CO-PILA-003
Report Matrix!A7	Document Title
Report Matrix!B7	Integrated Contribution Settlement Return (PILA) - Field Specification
Report Matrix!A8	Country
Report Matrix!B8	Colombia
Report Matrix!A9	Authority
Report Matrix!B9	Ministry of Health and Social Protection, filed THROUGH A LICENSED PRIVATE INFORMATION OPERATOR
Report Matrix!A10	Report Type
Report Matrix!B10	Statutory monthly social-security and payroll-levy settlement
Report Matrix!A11	Tax Type
Report Matrix!B11	Social security contributions and parafiscal payroll levies (not an income-tax filing)
Report Matrix!A12	Relationship
Report Matrix!B12	Employer to information operator (never directly to government)
Report Matrix!A13	Frequency
Report Matrix!B13	Monthly. Required while the employment relationship subsists, even with no payment in the month
Report Matrix!A14	Submission Deadline
Report Matrix!B14	2nd-16th BUSINESS DAY of the month following the period, by the LAST TWO DIGITS of the employer ID (Decree 780 of 2016 art. 3.2.2.1)
Report Matrix!A15	File Format
Report Matrix!B15	Two fixed-width files (per the operator's published layout)
Report Matrix!A16	File Extension
Report Matrix!B16	Not prescribed by the annex. The file extension is held in the information-operator configuration, as published by the chosen operador.
Report Matrix!A17	Character Encoding
Report Matrix!B17	Not prescribed by the annex. The character encoding is held in the information-operator configuration, as published by the chosen operador.
Report Matrix!A18	Record Types / Sections
Report Matrix!B18	Record Tipo 1 (Encabezado); Record Tipo 2 (Liquidacion)
Report Matrix!A19	Version
Report Matrix!B19	v1.0
Report Matrix!A22	DETAILED COMPLIANCE NOTES (Colombia-specific)
Report Matrix!A23	SUBMISSION CHANNEL
Report Matrix!A24	- Channel: file_upload (fixed-width) into a PORTAL operated by a licensed private information operator | Portal: the chosen operador's web site (the operador is licensed by the Ministry of Health and Social Protection; the employer selects one commercially) | Source: Res. MSPS 2388 de 2016 Technical Annex 2 s.0 and s.2.1.1.2.1, and Decree 780 of 2016 art. 3.2.3.9 as substituted by Decree 1990 of 2016
Report Matrix!A25	- THE SOCIAL-SECURITY CHANNEL IS NOT THE TAX CHANNEL. PILA never touches DIAN Muisca, and Form 350 never touches an information operator. Labelling either with the other's portal is a category error.
Report Matrix!A26	- Two modalities exist: 1 Electronica (the employer keys or uploads on the operador's site) and 2 Asistida (the operador's call centre keys it). Where Decree 780 of 2016 art. 3.2.3.9 para. 2 exceptionally permits assisted payment, the modality field must still say ELECTRONICA and the operador reports those employers to the Ministry through SISPRO structure PUB019EXPE by the fifth business day of the following month.
Report Matrix!A27	- The layout is byte-exact and published, so this Spec CAN and DOES carry a real fixed-width sample on Sheet 9 - generated programmatically from the position table so that the record lengths are exactly 567, 359 and 693 characters.
Report Matrix!A28	- Operator upload envelope: each operador wraps the two files in its own upload envelope and may add validations. The wrapper and any operador-specific validations are published by the chosen operador and held in the information-operator configuration; the two files inside the envelope follow the annex layout byte for byte.
Report Matrix!A29	INTEGRATION TYPE AND DIRECTION
Report Matrix!A30	- Integration: outbound file generation and inbound response handling. Outbound: the two fixed-width files. Inbound: the operador's settlement response.
Report Matrix!A31	- The inbound leg (CO-IN-PILA-SETTLEMENT) is specified here rather than in a separate workbook because it is the response half of one exchange and is keyed on this filing's own return number (T2R1-017). The engine must reconcile the settled contributions against its own computation, capture the return number and payment date, and detect rejected contributor records. Each operador's response layout is proprietary and is published by the operador itself - it has no published national standard, so this workbook carries no field-level specification for it; the chosen operador's layout is held in the information-operator configuration.
Report Matrix!A32	- product_mapping_required = true. PILA needs FIVE contribution bases per employee (pension, health, occupational risk, CCF, and the separate uncapped parafiscal base), FOUR day counts, and sixteen change-event flags with their date pairs. None of these can be derived at reporting time from a single gross figure.
Report Matrix!A33	- Owned upstream: the information operator's own file wrapper and portal. Mercans produces the two annex-conformant files; the operador owns everything after that.
Report Matrix!A34	RESIDENT / NON-RESIDENT TREATMENT
Report Matrix!A35	- PILA IS NOT A TAX FILING and carries NO residence concept. A non-resident employee working in Colombia under a Colombian contract contributes to social security on exactly the same terms as a resident - same bases, same rates, same ceiling and floor. The flat 20% of ET art. 408 has no application here.
Report Matrix!A36	- The one immigration-related field is 'Foreign national not liable to contribute to pensions' (T2R2-007), and it is about PENSION AFFILIATION, not tax residence: a foreign employee who proves affiliation to a pension system abroad may elect out of Colombian pension affiliation under Decree 1072 of 2015 art. 2.2.1.2.1.4. Health, occupational risk and the parafiscal levies remain fully payable.
Report Matrix!A37	- It follows that the same employee can be simultaneously (a) withheld at 20% on sheet 2 of Form 350 as a non-resident, and (b) reported in PILA on ordinary terms. Do not let the tax residence flag leak into the PILA population rules.
Report Matrix!A38	- The mirror case - 'Colombian abroad' (T2R2-008) - is also not a tax field. It suppresses the DIVIPOLA work-location fields and enables the foreign filing date in T2R2-097.
Report Matrix!A39	RELATED FILINGS / CROSS-REFERENCES
Report Matrix!A40	- The employee health, pension and solidarity-fund figures must reconcile to electronic payroll NIE163, NIE166 and NIE168 / NIE170 (CO-DIAN-001), allowing for the different rounding conventions - PILA rounds UP to the next 100, the XML uses two decimals round-half-to-even.
Report Matrix!A41	- The ET art. 114-1 exoneration in A1-033 and T2R2-076 rests on the SAME test as Form 350 casilla 27 (CO-DIAN-002). Configure it once.
Report Matrix!A42	- The employee's mandatory health and pension contributions - the amounts settled here - are the non-taxable income de renta that reduce the Form 350 casilla 77 base and are reported annually at Form 220 casillas 53-54 (CO-DIAN-004) and Format 2276 numerals 23-24 (CO-DIAN-005).
Report Matrix!A43	- PILA return types T and F (Sistema General de Participaciones) and types I and Y (independent workers) are out of scope - public sector and non-employees respectively. This specification covers the private-sector employer return.
Report Matrix!A44	- The occupational-risk premium (T2R2-063) and the employer's share of pension, health and the levies never appear on the electronic payroll, because that document reports only what the employee accrued and what was deducted from the employee.
Payroll Scope Summary!A1	PAYROLL SCOPE SUMMARY - INTEGRATED CONTRIBUTION SETTLEMENT RETURN (PILA) - SOCIAL SECURITY AND PAYROLL LEVIES
Payroll Scope Summary!A2	PURPOSE: Tell the developer WHO is reported on the monthly PILA settlement and what happens to a borderline case.
Payroll Scope Summary!A4	SCENARIO
Payroll Scope Summary!B4	REPORT?
Payroll Scope Summary!C4	NOTES / REASONING
Payroll Scope Summary!A5	═══ WHO GETS REPORTED ═══
Payroll Scope Summary!A6	Indefinite-term employee (indefinite-term contract)
Payroll Scope Summary!B6	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C6	The core private-sector population. CST art. 47; Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 5.
Payroll Scope Summary!A7	Fixed-term employee (fixed-term contract, max 4 years)
Payroll Scope Summary!B7	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C7	Identical reporting treatment to an indefinite-term contract. CST art. 46; Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 6.
Payroll Scope Summary!A8	Specific-work or task employee (contract por obra o labor)
Payroll Scope Summary!B8	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C8	An employment contract; CST art. 46. Electronic payroll TipoContrato = 3 (Obra o Labor).
Payroll Scope Summary!A9	Occasional / accidental / transitory worker (< 1 month, non-core activity)
Payroll Scope Summary!B9	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C9	Still an employee under CST art. 6. Reported on every filing; the PILA return type and the day counts differ, not the obligation.
Payroll Scope Summary!A10	Part-time employee
Payroll Scope Summary!B10	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C10	Colombia has no separate part-time regime. Reported normally; the PILA contribution base is floored at 1 SMMLV (COP 1,750,905) except for the worker type 51 partial-time regime.
Payroll Scope Summary!A11	All-inclusive salary employee (all-inclusive salary, >= 13 SMMLV)
Payroll Scope Summary!B11	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C11	CST art. 132 num. 2. Reported with the integral-salary flag set; the contribution base is 70% of the integral salary, and the electronic payroll carries SalarioIntegral = true.
Payroll Scope Summary!A12	Apprentice (apprenticeship contract)
Payroll Scope Summary!B12	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C12	Law 2466 of 2025 art. 21 converted the apprenticeship into a special fixed-term LABOUR contract, regulated by Decree 0223 of 5 March 2026. Electronic payroll TipoContrato = 4; PILA tipo cotizante 19 'Aprendices etapa lectiva Ley 2466 de 2025' for the lectiva phase from the health period of August 2025 (code 12 only for earlier periods). For health periods before August 2025, code 19 covers the productive phase; from the health period of August 2025, AT2 v30 names no code for the productive phase (AT2 v30 §2.1.2.3.1 campo 5, types 12 and 19).
Payroll Scope Summary!A13	Intern / practical placement (practicas o pasantias)
Payroll Scope Summary!B13	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C13	Electronic payroll TipoContrato = 5. Reported where remunerated.
Payroll Scope Summary!A14	Teleworkers and remote workers
Payroll Scope Summary!B14	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C14	Law 1221 of 2008 as amended by Law 2466 of 2025 arts. 52-54. No reporting difference; the electronic payroll carries a dedicated Teletrabajo earning element (NIE158).
Payroll Scope Summary!A15	Resident foreign national under a Colombian contract
Payroll Scope Summary!B15	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C15	Full reporting. Withheld under the ET art. 383 progressive table once tax residence is acquired.
Payroll Scope Summary!A16	NON-RESIDENT foreign national under a Colombian contract
Payroll Scope Summary!B16	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C16	IN SCOPE and frequently mis-configured. Withheld at the flat 20% of ET art. 408 on the GROSS, with no art. 388 depuration, until the 183-day residence test is met (DIAN Concept 011108 of 29 June 2026). See the resident / non-resident block in the Report Matrix.
Payroll Scope Summary!A17	Employed old-age pensioner in active service
Payroll Scope Summary!B17	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C17	PILA worker sub-type 01; no pension contribution (PILA pension rate field = 0), health and the levies still apply.
Payroll Scope Summary!A18	Executive director / statutory legal representative holding an employment contract
Payroll Scope Summary!B18	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C18	Reported as an ordinary employee where an employment contract exists.
Payroll Scope Summary!A19	Independent contractor (service contract)
Payroll Scope Summary!B19	NO
Payroll Scope Summary!C19	Not an employee. The contractor self-files PILA on 40% of fees (return types I / Y, descoped) and is reported by the payer in the third-party information return, not in this employer filing. Withholding on their fees does pass through Form 350, but under a different casilla.
Payroll Scope Summary!A20	Public-sector employee (contributor type 47, SGP-funded entity)
Payroll Scope Summary!B20	NO
Payroll Scope Summary!C20	Public-sector only; PILA return types T and F (Sistema General de Participaciones) are out of scope - this specification covers the private-sector employer return.
Payroll Scope Summary!A21	Non-executive director paid fees only (junta directiva)
Payroll Scope Summary!B21	NO
Payroll Scope Summary!C21	Not an employee: a director paid fees only holds no employment contract (an executive director who holds one is reported under the row 'Executive director / statutory legal representative holding an employment contract'), so there is no dependent-worker record to report in this employer filing. The fee recipient contributes as an independent on their own return (types I / Y, out of scope), the same treatment as the independent contractor.
Payroll Scope Summary!A22	Pensioner whose pension is paid by a fund, not by the employer
Payroll Scope Summary!B22	NO
Payroll Scope Summary!C22	The paying fund is the withholding agent and the reporting party, not the employer. A pensioner whose pension the employer pays directly is not reported in this return for that pension: the employer, as pension payer type 01 Empleador, reports the pensioner on the pensioners' PILA under Anexo Tecnico 3 'Aportes a Seguridad Social de Pensionados' (CO-PILA-007; Res. MSPS 2388 de 2016 art. 1, Anexo Tecnico 3; AT3 v15 §1.2.4, p. 13). If that pensioner also works for the employer, the employment is reported in this return (row 'Employed old-age pensioner in active service').
Payroll Scope Summary!A23	═══ AMOUNTS AND CONCEPTS REPORTED ═══
Payroll Scope Summary!A24	Salary and salary-constituting payments
Payroll Scope Summary!B24	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C24	The core of all five contribution bases (CST art. 127).
Payroll Scope Summary!A25	Pacted non-salary payments within the 40% clamp
Payroll Scope Summary!B25	NO
Payroll Scope Summary!C25	Payments agreed as non-salary under an express CST art. 128 pact are excluded from the contribution base - but only up to 40% of total remuneration. Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 adds the EXCESS over 40% back into the base, and that added-back amount must be separately identifiable because it is in the contribution base but out of the statutory-benefit base.
Payroll Scope Summary!A26	Statutory transport allowance
Payroll Scope Summary!B26	NO
Payroll Scope Summary!C26	Not salary for contribution purposes. It IS in the base of cesantias, prima and the severance indemnity - a different base, and one of the sharpest traps in Colombian payroll.
Payroll Scope Summary!A27	Paid rest days
Payroll Scope Summary!B27	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C27	In the CCF base (campo 45, T2R2-045) and in the PARAFISCAL base (campo 95, T2R2-095): Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17 defines one monthly salary payroll for the Subsidio Familiar (CCF), SENA, ESAP and Escuelas Industriales e Institutos Técnicos, which includes the payments 'verificados por descansos remunerados de ley y convencionales o contractuales'. Paid rest days include vacation enjoyed and vacation paid in cash, during employment or on termination (Consejo de Estado, Sala de Consulta, concept 2027 of 2010-10-04). The ICBF base is the same payroll (Ley 89 de 1988 art. 1 par. 1).
Payroll Scope Summary!A28	Contribution base ceiling of 25 SMMLV
Payroll Scope Summary!B28	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C28	COP 43,772,625 in 2026. Applies to pension, health and occupational risk only. Does NOT apply to the CCF base in campo 45 (monthly salary payroll, Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17; 70% for an integral salary, Ley 789 de 2002 art. 49) nor to the parafiscal base in campo 95 - both are uncapped.
Payroll Scope Summary!A29	Contribution base floor of 1 SMMLV
Payroll Scope Summary!B29	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C29	COP 1,750,905 in 2026, except for the partial-time regime (contributor type 51), which uses hours worked in campo 96.
Payroll Scope Summary!A30	All-inclusive salary at 70% of the integral salary
Payroll Scope Summary!B30	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C30	CST art. 132 num. 2. Flagged in campo 41 and applied to all four IBC fields.
Payroll Scope Summary!A31	Employer contributions
Payroll Scope Summary!B31	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C31	PILA is the ONLY artefact that reports the employer's cost - pension 12%, health 8.5%, occupational risk 0.348% to 8.7%, CCF 4%, SENA 2%, ICBF 3%.
Payroll Scope Summary!A32	Employee deductions
Payroll Scope Summary!B32	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C32	Pension 4%, health 4%, solidarity fund 1% to 2%. Reported as part of the total contribution, not as a separate deduction line.
Payroll Scope Summary!A33	Income tax withholding
Payroll Scope Summary!B33	NO
Payroll Scope Summary!C33	PILA is a contribution settlement, not a tax return. Withholding goes on Form 350.
Payroll Scope Summary!A34	SENA and ICBF for employees under 10 SMMLV where the employer is exonerated
Payroll Scope Summary!B34	NO
Payroll Scope Summary!C34	Where the employer flag A1-033 is S and the employee's monthly flag T2R2-076 is S - total devengado and health IBC both below 10 SMMLV (COP 17,509,050 in 2026) - the SENA 2% and ICBF 3% rates and values (T2R2-066 to T2R2-069) are reported as zero, together with the employer health 8.5% (ET art. 114-1; Decreto 1828 de 2013 art. 7; AT2 v30 campo 76). Nothing else is exonerated: pension, occupational risk and the CCF 4% in the row below remain payable in full.
Payroll Scope Summary!A35	CCF 4% for the same employees
Payroll Scope Summary!B35	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C35	The family compensation fund is NEVER exonerated under art. 114-1. The only relief is the Ley 1429 de 2010 art. 5 regime, flagged separately in A1-034.
Payroll Scope Summary!A36	═══ TIMING AND RELATIONSHIP ═══
Payroll Scope Summary!A37	Reported on the accrual (causacion) date
Payroll Scope Summary!B37	NO
Payroll Scope Summary!C37	Every Colombian payroll filing is keyed to the PAYMENT or credit-in-account date, not to the accrual date. A December salary paid in January falls in the January filing.
Payroll Scope Summary!A38	Reported on the payment or credit-in-account date
Payroll Scope Summary!B38	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C38	The universal Colombian rule - payment or credit in account. Applies to the electronic payroll (DIAN Resolution 000227 of 2025 art. 1.5.3.2.2), to Form 350, to Form 220 and to Format 2276.
Payroll Scope Summary!A39	A semi-monthly payroll produces two filings a month
Payroll Scope Summary!B39	NO
Payroll Scope Summary!C39	It does not. The electronic payroll is expressly ONE monthly document per employee 'irrespective of whether the obliged party makes payments in different or shorter periods' (Res. DIAN 000227 de 2025 art. 1.5.3.2.2). PILA and Form 350 are likewise monthly.
Payroll Scope Summary!A40	Nil period still filed
Payroll Scope Summary!B40	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C40	The return is required for every month in which the employment relationship subsists, even with no payment in the month (Report Matrix, Frequency; filing calendar in Decree 780 of 2016 art. 3.2.2.1). An employee with no pay in the month is reported with the change event that explains it - SLN with its date pair and reduced day counts, as in the row 'Employee on unpaid leave for the whole month'.
Payroll Scope Summary!A41	Corrections handled by re-filing the whole period
Payroll Scope Summary!B41	VARIES
Payroll Scope Summary!C41	Electronic payroll: NO - a dedicated adjustment note (NominaIndividualDeAjuste, Replace or Delete) referencing the original CUNE. Form 350: yes, a corrective return. PILA: a correction return type N (correcciones) for the period, filed through the same operador as the original return, unless the Superintendencia Financiera has authorised that operador's wind-down; the operador settles the C records (correct values) minus the A records (original values), the C records must be equal to or greater than the A records in IBC and in days, and type N cannot be used for a correction that leads to a refund, which is requested from each administradora outside PILA (AT2 v30 §2.1.1.2.2 campo 8; Disposiciones comunes a la Planilla N; §2.1.2.3.9 campo 22). Return type M (mora) settles only periods before health period March 2014 with a total absence of payment; a correction that raises a value is made on type N, not on type M (AT2 v30 §2.1.1.2.2 campo 8, type M).
Payroll Scope Summary!A42	═══ SPECIAL CASES ═══
Payroll Scope Summary!A43	Health period one month ahead of the other subsystems
Payroll Scope Summary!B43	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C43	T2R1-016 normally carries the month AFTER T2R1-015: health is paid one month in advance. Setting them equal is the classic first-run defect.
Payroll Scope Summary!A44	Employee on unpaid leave for the whole month
Payroll Scope Summary!B44	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C44	Reported with the SLN flag, its date pair, and reduced day counts. The employment relationship subsists, so the return is still filed.
Payroll Scope Summary!A45	Employed old-age pensioner
Payroll Scope Summary!B45	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C45	Contributor sub-type 01. The annex forces the pension rate in campo 46 to zero for sub-types 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9 and 12.
Payroll Scope Summary!A46	Foreign employee with proven pension cover abroad
Payroll Scope Summary!B46	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C46	Flagged in campo 7. Pension is waived; health, occupational risk and the levies remain.
Payroll Scope Summary!A47	Employee at more than one work centre with different risk classes
Payroll Scope Summary!B47	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C47	The class attaches to the WORK CENTRE (Decree 1072 of 2015 art. 2.2.4.2.5.6). Where a person performs several occupations they contribute at the HIGHEST class; a mid-period move is reported through the VCT flag and its date pair.
Payroll Scope Summary!A48	Contributor type 40 (Beneficiary UPC Adicional)
Payroll Scope Summary!B48	PARTIAL
Payroll Scope Summary!C48	Reported as a detail record but EXCLUDED from the header contributor count in T2R1-019, and with its DIVIPOLA work-location fields blank.
Payroll Scope Summary!A49	Late payment
Payroll Scope Summary!B49	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C49	A return paid after its deadline is filed on the ordinary return type E and the operador computes the interest on arrears (AT2 v30 §2.1.1.2.2 campo 8, type E). Return type M (mora) applies only to periods before health period March 2014 with a total absence of payment, and type N to a return already paid that is amended; T2R1-009 and T2R1-010 are blank on types E and M and carry the associated return number and date on type N (AT2 v30 §2.1.1.1 campos 9 and 10).

PILA Spec!K4	MERCANS COLOMBIA S.A.S.
PILA Spec!L4	Company.Legal_Name
PILA Spec!M4	Registered name of the contributing employer. Supplied by the aportante and echoed into File Tipo 2 Record Tipo 1 campo 4, where the annex says expressly 'El registrado en el campo 1 del archivo tipo 1' - the two must be byte-identical. Source: the registered legal name of the employer as in the RUT / commercial registry, held in company configuration. Do NOT take the HR Blizz 'Organization' label, because it prefixes the internal company code to the name.
PILA Spec!N4	Y
PILA Spec!O4	-
PILA Spec!P4	Not an amount - no rounding.
PILA Spec!Q4	N
PILA Spec!R4	Text
PILA Spec!S4	Monthly
PILA Spec!A5	Employer document type
PILA Spec!B5	Tipo documento del aportante
PILA Spec!C5	File Type 1 - Contributor
PILA Spec!D5	A1-002
PILA Spec!E5	Y
PILA Spec!F5	Y
PILA Spec!H5	Pos 201-202
PILA Spec!I5	2
PILA Spec!J5	A(2) - alphanumeric, left-justified, SPACE-filled to the right
PILA Spec!K5	NI
PILA Spec!L5	Company.Document_Type
PILA Spec!M5	Identity-document type of the employer, from the closed list NI, CC, CE, TI, PA, CD, SC, PE, PT (PT = Permiso por Protección Temporal). PE may not be used for health contribution periods from September 2023 onwards. For a corporate employer it is always NI (tax identification number). Echoed into T2R1 campo 5. Source: AT2 v30 (MinSalud, Res. 2388/2016 Anexo Técnico 2, version 30, 24-07-2026) §1.1 campo 2.
PILA Spec!N5	Y
PILA Spec!O5	-> Sheet 6: Tipo document contributor
PILA Spec!P5	Not an amount - no rounding.
PILA Spec!Q5	N
PILA Spec!R5	Code
PILA Spec!S5	Monthly
PILA Spec!A6	Employer identification number
PILA Spec!B6	Numero de Identificacion del aportante
PILA Spec!C6	File Type 1 - Contributor
PILA Spec!D6	A1-003
PILA Spec!E6	Y
PILA Spec!F6	Y
PILA Spec!H6	Pos 203-218
PILA Spec!I6	16
PILA Spec!J6	A(16) - alphanumeric, left-justified, SPACE-filled to the right
PILA Spec!K6	900123456
PILA Spec!L6	Company.Document_Number
PILA Spec!M6	The employer's identification number. The information operator validates that it contains only A-Z and 0-9. An ALPHANUMERIC number is permitted ONLY for PA (passport, up to 16) and CD (carne diplomatico, up to 11). PILA Spec!M36	S or N. THE SINGLE MOST CONSEQUENTIAL FLAG IN THE EMPLOYER RECORD. It declares that the employer is exonerated under ET art. 114-1 from the EMPLOYER health contribution of 8.5%, from the SENA levy of 2% and from the ICBF levy of 3%, for employees earning less than 10 SMMLV (COP 17,509,050 in 2026). PILA Spec!L57	COUNT(Registro Tipo 2 records)
PILA Spec!M57	Must equal the number of UNIQUE contributors in the Record Tipo 2 detail, EXCLUDING those whose 'Tipo contributor' (T2R2-005) is 40 - Beneficiary UPC Adicional. PILA Spec!L65	Employee.Document_Type
PILA Spec!M65	Closed list CC, CE, TI, PA, CD, SC, RC, PE, PT (PT = Permiso por Protección Temporal). PE may not be used for health contribution periods from September 2023 onwards. An ALPHANUMERIC identification number is permitted only for PA and CD; for TI, CC, SC, RC, CE, PE and PT the number must be all digits. NOTE the divergence from the electronic payroll, which uses NUMERIC codes (13 = CC) rather than these two-letter codes - the engine must MAP, not copy. Bidirectional set with 'Foreign national not liable to contribute to pension' (T2R2-007), which where marked restricts this field to CE, PA, CD, SC, PE or PT, and with 'Colombian abroad' (T2R2-008), which is used only where this field is CC or TI. COMPOSITE KEY: together with T2R2-004 (Contributor identification number), this pair is the join key for the contributor across every Colombian artefact -- electronic payroll NIE044/NIE045, Form 220 casillas 24/25, Format 2276 numeral 2 -- matching the same pair convention documented on CO-CESANTIAS-006 T03/T04. Source: AT2 v30 (MinSalud, Res. 2388/2016 Anexo Técnico 2, version 30, 24-07-2026) §2.1.2.1 campos 3, 7 and 8. Source: Master Data holds no document-type field. Derive the type from the populated document column: 'Citizenship Card ID' -> CC; 'Indentity Card for Foreigners' -> CE. The document-column to PILA document-type mapping - the PILA code of each document column, including 'National ID' and 'Identity Card', and the precedence where more than one column is populated - is maintained as a configuration table. An employee with no document column populated is rejected as a blocking data error.

PILA Spec!A78	ING - hire

PILA Spec!C78	File Type 2, Record Type 2 - Settlement

PILA Spec!G78	Change-event flag, CONDITIONAL: blank is permitted by the layout; populated only in the period in which the hire (entry into the contribution system) occurs, and only for the employee it affects. Permitted values are blank, R, X or C (AT2 v30 (MinSalud, Res. 2388/2016 Anexo Técnico 2, version 30, 24-07-2026) campo 15); for a dependent employee only X applies. The whole change event block T2R2-015 to T2R2-030 is mutually constraining: the flag drives which of the date fields T2R2-080 to T2R2-097 are populated, and it drives the day counts in T2R2-036 to T2R2-039. Where marked, the engine populates 'Hire date' (T2R2-080) with the hire date (AT2 v30 campo 80 makes the date optional except for contributor type 75; the engine's own interlock requires it).

PILA Spec!A79	RET - termination
PILA Spec!B79	RET: Retiro
PILA Spec!C79	File Type 2, Record Type 2 - Settlement
PILA Spec!D79	T2R2-016
PILA Spec!E79	N
PILA Spec!F79	C
PILA Spec!G79	Change-event flag, CONDITIONAL: blank is permitted by the layout; populated only in the period in which the termination occurs, and only for the employee it affects. Permitted values are blank, P, R, X, C or T (AT2 v30 (MinSalud, Res. 2388/2016 Anexo Técnico 2, version 30, 24-07-2026) campo 16, position 138); for a dependent employee only X applies, and T is reported by the contracting party for independent contributors of type 59 in the planilla type Y, never for an employee. The whole change event block T2R2-015 to T2R2-030 is mutually constraining: the flag drives which of the date fields T2R2-080 to T2R2-097 are populated, and it drives the day counts in T2R2-036 to T2R2-039. Where marked, the engine populates 'Termination date' (T2R2-081) with the termination date (AT2 v30 campo 81 makes the date optional when RET is reported; the engine's own interlock requires it).
PILA Spec!H79	Pos 138-138
PILA Spec!I79	1
PILA Spec!J79	A(1) - alphanumeric, left-justified, SPACE-filled to the right
PILA Spec!L79	Payroll.Novedad_RET [Not an amount field -- a PILA novedad (change-event) code; no WTC code applies by design]
PILA Spec!M79	Marks the employee's exit from the systems settled in the return. AT2 v30 (MinSalud, Res. 2388/2016 Anexo Técnico 2, version 30, 24-07-2026) campo 16 (position 138, one character supplied by the contributor - 'suministrado por el aportante') admits blank, P, R, X, C or T. For a dependent employee the value is X (withdrawal from all the systems settled) in the period in which the termination date falls, and blank in every other period; P reports a withdrawal from the General Pension System only, and R and C apply to independent contributors. T is reported by the contracting party for independent contributors of type 59 in the planilla type Y and never applies to an employee. Pairs with the termination date in campo 81 (T2R2-081), optional under AT2 v30 when RET is reported and always populated by the engine, and drives the contributory days in campos 36 to 39, which run from the first day of the period to the termination date inclusive on the 30-day commercial month (a termination on the last calendar day of the month reports 30 days); the occupational-risk days follow the campo 38 rule (T2R2-038). Source: the termination date of the contract (Contract.End_Date), the same date as the final settlement's termination date and the electronic payroll FechaRetiro (NIE003). A termination processed after the return of its period was paid is reported for that period, not in the period in which the final settlement is paid, through a correction return (T2R1-008 = N) filed with the same operador that settled the original return, and only where the correction does not lower the contribution: the sum of IBC and the sum of days in the C records must each be equal to or greater than those in the A records, and return type N cannot be used for a correction that leads to a refund of amounts paid in excess (AT2 v30 §2.1.1.2.2 campo 8, type N, numerals 1 and 2; Disposiciones comunes a la Planilla N). A RET omitted from the original return of the same settlement period and filed on type N by the last business day of the month in which payment is due carries no interest on arrears (AT2 v30 type N numeral 2; section 16 'Manejo de planilla tipo N' item h). Where reporting the termination lowers the days or the IBC already paid, PILA is not the channel: the refund ('devolución o reintegro de aportes') is requested from each administradora that received the contributions.

PILA Spec!L108	Payroll.IBC_Pension [WTC: CO-BASE-IBC]
PILA Spec!M108	Contribution base for pension. FLOOR 1 SMMLV = COP 1,750,905; CEILING 25 SMMLV = COP 43,772,625 in 2026. Must reconcile to electronic payroll NIE166 divided by the rate in T2R2-046, allowing for the different rounding conventions. Bidirectional set with 'All-inclusive salary flag' (T2R2-041), which where marked sets this base to 70% of the all-inclusive salary, and with 'Solidarity pension fund - solidarity sub-account' (T2R2-051), which is payable only where this base reaches 4 SMMLV. Computation order: (a) salary-constituting pay of the period (70% of it for an integral salary); (b) plus the excess of pacted non-salary payments over 40% of TOTAL remuneration (Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30); (c) floor 1 SMMLV, ceiling 25 SMMLV; (d) round UP to the whole peso (the payroll intermediate value may carry decimals, e.g. 17300699.5). Source: payroll field 'IBC Total' (the base on which WT 2556 Pension Plan Mandatory - AFP Employee, 4%, is computed); WT 64995 'Non-SSI excess' holds the Ley 1393 de 2010 add-back.

PILA Spec!L109	Payroll.IBC_Health [WTC: CO-BASE-IBC]
PILA Spec!M109	Contribution base for health. Same floor and ceiling as pension. The four IBC values legitimately DIFFER from one another once a non-salary payment, a change event or a subsystem-specific rule enters - do not populate them from one variable. Bidirectional pair with 'Contributor exonerated from payment' (T2R2-076): where this base is at or above 10 SMMLV (COP 17,509,050 in 2026) T2R2-076 MUST be N (AT2 v30 campo 76); where it is below 10 SMMLV, T2R2-076 is S only if the total earnings of the month (total devengado) are also below 10 SMMLV (ET art. 114-1; Decreto 1828 de 2013 art. 7) - this base alone does not grant the exoneration. Same computation order as T2R2-042 (70% integral, Ley 1393 add-back, floor and ceiling, round UP to the peso). Source: payroll field 'IBC Total', the base on which both the employee 4% (WT 2554) and the employer 8.5% (WT 2564) health contributions are computed.

No occupational-risk contribution is due for enjoyed vacation (VAC-LR = X) (Decreto 1072 de 2015 art. 2.2.4.2.1.6 numeral 1.3; Consejo de Estado, Sección Cuarta, exp. 25958 of 2022); remunerated leave (VAC-LR = L) remains contributory.

PILA Spec!L111	Payroll.IBC_CCF [WTC: CO-BASE-IBC]
PILA Spec!M111	Contribution base for the family compensation fund: the monthly salary payroll of the employee (Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17). Same floor (1 SMMLV = COP 1,750,905 in 2026) as the other three bases, but NO 25-SMMLV ceiling - the ceiling applies to the pension, health and occupational-risk IBC only. Bidirectional pair with 'All-inclusive salary flag' (T2R2-041), which where marked sets this base to 70% of the all-inclusive salary (Ley 789 de 2002 art. 49). Source: the CCF base computed by the payroll engine under this rule (wage type 64991 'Parafiscals Base actual', uncapped, is the base on which the CCF 4%, wage type 25110 Family Compensation Fund, is computed). Campo 45 (this field) and campo 95 (T2R2-095) are two separate bases and may legitimately differ: campo 45 is the base for the CCF contribution (AT2 v30 campo 65 = campo 45 x campo 64) and campo 95 the base for SENA, ICBF, ESAP and MEN (campos 67, 69, 71 and 73). Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17 defines the monthly salary payroll for the CCF and SENA alike and includes paid rest days ('descansos remunerados de ley y convencionales o contractuales'), so paid rest days enter both campo 45 and campo 95 - including vacation paid in cash, during employment or on termination (Consejo de Estado, Sala de Consulta, concept 2027 of 2010), which by contrast does NOT enter the pension, health and occupational-risk IBC (Consejo de Estado, Sección Cuarta, exp. 26941 of 2024; unification ruling exp. 25185 of 2021, rule 1). The engine stores and reports them independently, never deriving one from the other.

PILA Spec!F150	C
PILA Spec!G150	Marked where THIS EMPLOYEE, in THIS MONTH, earns less than 10 SMMLV (COP 17,509,050 in 2026) and the employer has declared itself exonerated in File Tipo 1 campo 33. Bidirectional set with A1-033 and with the SENA and ICBF rate and value fields T2R2-066 to T2R2-069 and the health rate T2R2-054, all of which must be consistent with it. HARD RULE printed at annex s.2.1.2.3.39: where 'Health contribution base' (T2R2-043) is at or above 10 SMMLV this field MUST be N - and likewise where a NATURAL-person employer employs two or more workers and the health base reaches 10 SMMLV.
PILA Spec!H150	Pos 506-506
PILA Spec!I150	1
PILA Spec!J150	A(1) - alphanumeric, left-justified, SPACE-filled to the right
PILA Spec!K150	S
PILA Spec!L150	Payroll.Exoneration_Flag_Monthly [WTC: CO-FLAG-EXONERACION-114-1]
PILA Spec!M150	THE PER-EMPLOYEE, PER-MONTH SIDE of the ET art. 114-1 exoneration (introduced by Ley 1607 de 2012 and confirmed by Ley 1819 de 2016 art. 65). Values are S and N, not X. The employer-level flag in A1-033 declares eligibility; THIS field applies it to a specific employee in a specific month. TWO RULES APPLY TOGETHER. (1) The exoneration test is on the TOTAL EARNINGS of the month (total devengado): the employee is exonerated where the total devengado of the month is below 10 SMMLV (COP 17,509,050 in 2026) - Decreto 1828 de 2013 art. 7. It is an earnings test, NOT a test on the health IBC. (2) In addition, AT2 v30 campo 76 requires N whenever the health contribution base in campo 43 (T2R2-043) is at or above 10 SMMLV. The field is therefore S only where BOTH the total devengado and the health IBC are below 10 SMMLV. The test is monthly, so an employee who crosses 10 SMMLV because of a bonus loses the exoneration for that month alone - the engine must evaluate it every period, never once a year. Where marked S, the employer's health 8.5%, SENA 2% and ICBF 3% fall away and the health rate in T2R2-054 becomes the EMPLOYEE rate alone (4%); pension, occupational risk and the CCF 4% remain payable in full. The total devengado is the totality of what the worker receives in the month - every payment of the period, salary and non-salary alike, whatever its character (Decreto 1828 de 2013 art. 7); the engine sums all earnings paid in the month without excluding any of them.
PILA Spec!N150	Y
PILA Spec!O150	-> Sheet 6: Exoneracion art. 114-1
PILA Spec!P150	Not an amount - no rounding.
PILA Spec!Q150	N
PILA Spec!R150	Flag
PILA Spec!S150	Monthly

File Structure!A1	FILE STRUCTURE AND TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS
File Structure!A2	═══ SUBMISSION FORMAT ═══
File Structure!A3	Channel
File Structure!B3	file_upload of two fixed-width flat files into the web portal of a LICENSED PRIVATE INFORMATION OPERATOR, or direct keying / call-centre entry on the same portal. The employer NEVER transacts directly with the government for social security.
File Structure!A4	Portal
File Structure!B4	The information operator chosen commercially by the employer. Operadores are licensed by the Ministry of Health and Social Protection. THIS IS NOT THE DIAN PORTAL: PILA never touches Muisca and Form 350 never touches an operador.
File Structure!A5	Source for the channel
File Structure!B5	Res. MSPS 2388 de 2016 Technical Annex 2, s.0 (File description) and s.2.1.1.2.1 (Return type); Decree 780 of 2016 art. 3.2.3.9 as substituted by Decree 1990 of 2016.
File Structure!A6	Modalities
File Structure!B6	1 Electronica - the employer keys or uploads on the operador's web site. 2 Asistida - the operador's call centre keys it. Where Decree 780 art. 3.2.3.9 para. 2 exceptionally permits assisted payment, the field must still read ELECTRONICA and the operador reports those employers to the Ministry through SISPRO structure PUB019EXPE by the fifth business day of the following month.
File Structure!A7	Operator upload wrapper
File Structure!B7	The two files below are byte-exact against the official annex. Each operador's upload wrapper and any additional validations it layers on top are not publicly specified; they are published by each operador and held in the information-operator configuration for the operador the employer has chosen.
File Structure!A8	═══ FILE FORMAT ═══
File Structure!A9	File type
File Structure!B9	Two fixed-width flat text files, one record per line.
File Structure!A10	Numeric fields (tipo N)
File Structure!B10	Right-justified, ZERO-filled to the left. The annex states this expressly at s.0.
File Structure!A11	Alphanumeric fields (tipo A)
File Structure!B11	Left-justified, SPACE-filled to the right.
File Structure!A12	Record lengths
File Structure!B12	File Tipo 1 = 567 positions. File Tipo 2 Record Tipo 1 = 359 positions. File Tipo 2 Record Tipo 2 = 693 positions. All three equal the printed totals of AT2 v30 (24-07-2026).
File Structure!A13	Rounding
File Structure!B13	ROUND UP throughout. The contribution base is rounded up to the whole peso and each contribution value is rounded up to the NEXT MULTIPLE OF 100 (Decree 1990 of 2016 art. 1). This is a different convention from the electronic payroll (2 decimals, round-half-to-even) and from Form 350 (nearest 1,000). The same contribution therefore appears as different numbers in different artefacts.
File Structure!A14	Annual refresh obligation
File Structure!B14	The annex requires the employer to update ALL File Tipo 1 information with the operador from January 2018 onward, and to refresh it ANNUALLY. The operador must prompt for it.
File Structure!A15	═══ RECORD STRUCTURE ═══
File Structure!A16	File Tipo 1
File Structure!B16	Datos generales del aportante. 34 campos, positions 1 to 567. ONE record per employer (or per branch where the form of presentation is S).
File Structure!A17	File Tipo 2, Record Tipo 1
File Structure!B17	Encabezado. 22 campos, positions 1 to 359. ONE record. Identified by record type 01 in positions 1-2.
File Structure!A18	File Tipo 2, Record Tipo 2
File Structure!B18	Detailed contribution settlement. 98 campos, positions 1 to 693. ONE record PER CONTRIBUTOR. Identified by record type 02 in positions 1-2, with a strictly sequential counter in positions 3-7 starting at 00001.
File Structure!A19	Records Tipo 3 to 12
File Structure!B19	TOTALS RECORDS GENERATED BY THE OPERADOR, not by the employer. Type 3 total pension contributions; type 4 solidarity pension fund; type 5 health; type 6 occupational risk; type 7 family compensation funds; type 8 SENA; type 9 ICBF; type 10 ESAP; type 11 MEN; type 12 total payable for the period. They also carry the days-in-arrears count and the interest on arrears, which the operador computes from the payment date. The employer neither produces nor validates them, which is why they are OUT OF SCOPE for this Spec - but the engine must PARSE them from the settlement response to reconcile.
File Structure!A20	═══ SECTION LAYOUT - FILE TIPO 1 (34 CAMPOS, 567 POSICIONES) ═══
File Structure!A21	Identification
File Structure!B21	1 Nombre/razon social (1-200) | 2 Tipo documento (201-202) | 3 Numero de identificacion (203-218) | 4 Digito de verificacion (219)

File Structure!B22	5 Branch code (220-229) | 6 Nombre sucursal (230-269)

File Structure!A25	Legal representative
File Structure!B25	18 Numero identification (403-418) | 19 Digito verificacion (419) | 20 Tipo identification (420-421) | 21 First surname (422-441) | 22 Second surname (442-471) | 23 First given name (472-491) | 24 Segundo nombre (492-521)
File Structure!A26	Insolvency
File Structure!B26	25 Fecha inicio concordato/reestructuracion/liquidacion/cese (522-531) | 26 Tipo de accion (532) | 27 Fecha fin actividades comerciales (533-542)

File Structure!A29	═══ SECTION LAYOUT - FILE TIPO 2, RECORD TIPO 1 (22 CAMPOS, 359 POSICIONES) ═══
File Structure!A30	Header
File Structure!B30	1 Tipo de registro = 01 (1-2) | 2 Modalidad de la planilla (3) | 3 Secuencia (4-7)
File Structure!A31	Employer echo
File Structure!B31	4 Nombre/razon social (8-207) | 5 Tipo document (208-209) | 6 Numero identification (210-225) | 7 Digito verificacion (226) - all four must equal File Tipo 1 campos 1, 2, 3 and 4
File Structure!A32	Return identity
File Structure!B32	8 Return type (227) | 9 Numero associated return (228-237) | 10 Fecha payment associated return (238-247)
File Structure!A33	Branch and ARL
File Structure!B33	11 Forma de presentacion (248) | 12 Branch code (249-258) | 13 Nombre sucursal (259-298) | 14 Code ARL (299-304)
File Structure!A34	Periods
File Structure!B34	15 Periodo de pago para los sistemas diferentes al de salud (305-311) | 16 PERIODO DE PAGO PARA EL SISTEMA DE SALUD (312-318) - normally the month AFTER campo 15, because health is paid one month in advance
File Structure!A35	Settlement
File Structure!B35	17 Numero de radicacion o de la Planilla Integrada de Liquidacion de Aportes (319-328) | 18 Fecha de pago (329-338) | 19 Numero total de cotizantes reportados en esta planilla (339-343) | 20 Valor total de la nomina - the sum of campo 45 IBC CCF (344-355) | 21 Tipo de aportante (356-357) | 22 Codigo del operador de informacion (358-359)
File Structure!A36	═══ SECTION LAYOUT - FILE TIPO 2, RECORD TIPO 2 (98 CAMPOS, 693 POSICIONES) ═══
File Structure!A37	Identity (1-14)
File Structure!B37	1 Tipo de registro = 02 (1-2) | 2 Secuencia (3-7) | 3 Tipo documento (8-9) | 4 Numero identificacion (10-25) | 5 Tipo cotizante (26-27) | 6 Subtipo (28-29) | 7 Extranjero no obligado a cotizar a pensiones (30) | 8 Colombiano en el exterior (31) | 9 Departamento laboral (32-33) | 10 Municipio laboral (34-36) | 11-14 Apellidos y nombres (37-136)
File Structure!A38	Change events (15-30)
File Structure!B38	15 ING (137) | 16 RET (138) | 17 TDE (139) | 18 TAE (140) | 19 TDP (141) | 20 TAP (142) | 21 VSP (143) | 22 Correcciones (144) | 23 VST (145) | 24 SLN (146) | 25 IGE (147) | 26 LMA (148) | 27 VAC-LR (149) | 28 AVP (150) | 29 VCT (151) | 30 IRL dias (152-153)
File Structure!A39	Administradoras (31-35)
File Structure!B39	31 AFP del afiliado (154-159) | 32 AFP destino (160-165) | 33 EPS del afiliado (166-171) | 34 EPS destino (172-177) | 35 CCF (178-183)

File Structure!A42	Pension (46-53)
File Structure!B42	46 Rate pension (238-244) | 47 Compulsory contribution (245-253) | 48 Voluntary contribution affiliate (254-262) | 49 Voluntary contribution contributor (263-271) | 50 Total SGP (272-280) | 51 FSP solidaridad (281-289) | 52 FSP subsistencia (290-298) | 53 Value no retenido (299-307)
File Structure!A43	Salud (54-60)

File Structure!A45	Parafiscales (64-73)
File Structure!B45	64 Rate CCF (408-414) | 65 Value CCF (415-423) | 66 Rate SENA (424-430) | 67 Value SENA (431-439) | 68 Rate ICBF (440-446) | 69 Value ICBF (447-455) | 70 Rate ESAP (456-462) | 71 Value ESAP (463-471) | 72 Rate MEN (472-478) | 73 Value MEN (479-487)
File Structure!A46	Principal y exoneracion (74-79)
File Structure!B46	74 Tipo de documento del cotizante principal (488-489) | 75 Numero de identificacion del cotizante principal (490-505) | 76 COTIZANTE EXONERADO DE PAGO DE APORTE SALUD, SENA E ICBF (506) | 77 ARL del afiliado (507-512) | 78 Clase de riesgo en la que se encuentra el afiliado (513) | 79 Indicador tarifa especial pensiones (514)
File Structure!A47	Change-event dates (80-94)
File Structure!B47	80 Income (515-524) | 81 Retiro (525-534) | 82 Inicio VSP (535-544) | 83 Inicio SLN (545-554) | 84 Fin SLN (555-564) | 85 Inicio IGE (565-574) | 86 Fin IGE (575-584) | 87 Inicio LMA (585-594) | 88 Fin LMA (595-604) | 89 Inicio VAC-LR (605-614) | 90 Fin VAC-LR (615-624) | 91 Inicio VCT (625-634) | 92 Fin VCT (635-644) | 93 Inicio IRL (645-654) | 94 Fin IRL (655-664)
File Structure!A48	Cierre (95-97)
File Structure!B48	95 IBC OTROS PARAFISCALES DIFERENTES A CCF (665-673) | 96 Numero de horas laboradas (674-676) | 97 Fecha radicacion en el exterior (677-686) | 98 Actividad economica para el Sistema General de Riesgos Laborales (687-693)
File Structure!A49	═══ INBOUND - SETTLEMENT RESPONSE (INTEGRATION CO-IN-PILA-SETTLEMENT) ═══
File Structure!A50	What comes back
File Structure!B50	The operador returns a settlement: the return number (matching T2R1-017), the payment date, the totals records (types 3 to 12) and any rejected contributor records.
File Structure!A51	What the engine must do
File Structure!B51	Reconcile the settled contributions against its own computation per subsystem; capture the return number and payment date as proof of compliance; and detect rejected contributor records so they can be re-filed on a correction return (type N).
File Structure!A52	Why no field-level spec here
File Structure!B52	Each operador's response layout is PROPRIETARY. There is no published national standard for it, so writing a field-level specification would be invention. The layout is published by the chosen information operator and held in the information-operator configuration. This is an upstream-owned interface: the operador owns the response layout; the engine owns the reconciliation, capture and rejection handling described above.

Field Options!A3	═══ CLASE DE APORTANTE (A1-7) ═══
Field Options!A4	A
Field Options!B4	200+
Field Options!C4	Aportante con 200 o mas cotizantes
Field Options!D4	Counted across ALL branches and dependencies, not per branch.
Field Options!A5	B
Field Options!B5	<200
Field Options!C5	Aportante con menos de 200 cotizantes
Field Options!D5	Counted across all branches. The default for most corporate employers.
Field Options!A6	C
Field Options!B6	MIPYME
Field Options!C6	Aportante Mipyme que se acoge a Ley 590 de 2000
Field Options!D6	Fewer than 200 contributors AND eligible for the SENA, ICBF and CCF parafiscal discounts of Law 590 of 2000 and Decree 525 of 2009. The operador must verify MONTHLY that the employer appears in the file DIAN sends the Ministry under Decree 525 art. 6, checking the fechinst date - so the class cannot simply be asserted by the employer.
Field Options!A7	D
Field Options!B7	LEY1429
Field Options!C7	Aportante beneficiario de la Ley de Formalizacion y Generacion de Empleo
Field Options!D7	Beneficiary of Law 1429 of 2010 under Decree 1074 of 2015 art. 2.2.2.42.1. Requires the commercial-registry date in A1-031, which must fall between 29 December 2010 and 31 December 2014.
Field Options!A8	I
Field Options!B8	IND
Field Options!C8	Independiente
Field Options!D8	A natural person paying their own or their dependants' contributions. Not used by a corporate employer (AT2 v30 (24-07-2026) campo 7).

Population Rules!A1	POPULATION RULES - HOW TO FILL EACH FIELD FROM PAYROLL
Population Rules!A2	Field Name
Population Rules!B2	Source System
Population Rules!C2	Population Logic
Population Rules!D2	Edge Cases
Population Rules!A3	═══ MANDATORY FIELD RULES ═══
Population Rules!A4	The five contribution bases (T2R2-042 to T2R2-045 and T2R2-095)
Population Rules!B4	Payroll engine
Population Rules!C4	Compute and STORE five separate bases per employee per month. Three are the IBC for pension, health and occupational risk: total salary-constituting payments, plus the excess over the 40% non-salary clamp of Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30, floored at 1 SMMLV (COP 1,750,905) and capped at 25 SMMLV (COP 43,772,625). Vacation paid in cash (during employment or on termination) is NOT a salary-constituting payment and is excluded from these three IBCs (Consejo de Estado, Sección Cuarta, exp. 26941 of 2024; unification ruling exp. 25185 of 2021, rule 1); for enjoyed vacation days, health and pension are contributed on the IBC of the month before the vacation starts (DUR 780 de 2016 art. 3.2.5.1; Consejo de Estado, Sección Cuarta, exp. 25958 of 2022). The fourth is the IBC CCF of campo 45: the monthly salary payroll of Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17, which INCLUDES paid rest days (70% of the integral salary, Ley 789 de 2002 art. 49), floored at 1 SMMLV and NOT capped at 25 SMMLV. The fifth is the PARAFISCAL base of campo 95, defined by the same Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17: it INCLUDES paid rest days and it is UNCAPPED. Never populate all five from one variable.
Population Rules!D4	The four IBC values legitimately differ from one another once a change event enters - for example health may remain payable during an unpaid leave where pension does not. The header total in T2R1-020 is the sum of campo 45 (IBC CCF), NOT of campo 95 (AT2 v30 §2.1.1.1 campo 20); do not derive it from the parafiscal base.
Population Rules!A5	The four day counts (T2R2-036 to T2R2-039)
Population Rules!B5	Payroll engine
Population Rules!C5	Maintain a contributory-day counter PER SUBSYSTEM on the 30-day commercial month. Start at 30 (or the proportion from a mid-month hire or to a mid-month leave), then apply the change events: SLN and lawful strike reduce all four; IGE and IRL follow the subsystem-specific rules; vacation and remunerated leave do not reduce the day counters (AT2 v30 §2.1.2.3.11 to §2.1.2.3.13), although no occupational-risk contribution is due for enjoyed vacation days (Decreto 1072 de 2015 art. 2.2.4.2.1.6 numeral 1.3; Consejo de Estado, Sección Cuarta, exp. 25958 of 2022).
Population Rules!D5	February is 30 days and a 31-day month is 30 days. The counts must reconcile to the change event date pairs: a five-day SLN in a full month gives 25 contributory days and dates in T2R2-083 and T2R2-084 that span exactly five days.
Population Rules!A6	Health period one month ahead (T2R1-016)
Population Rules!B6	Engine (control)
Population Rules!C6	Set the health payment period to the month AFTER the period in T2R1-015. Health is paid one month in advance; the other subsystems are paid in arrears. A March salary settlement carries period 2026-03 in T2R1-015 and 2026-04 in T2R1-016.
Population Rules!D6	Setting the two equal is the single most common first-run defect. Note also the split of responsibility the annex records: T2R1-015 is CALCULATED BY THE OPERADOR, while T2R1-016 is SUPPLIED BY THE EMPLOYER.
Population Rules!A7	Exoneration flags (A1-033 and T2R2-076)
Population Rules!B7	Engine (control)
Population Rules!C7	A1-033 is an EMPLOYER-LEVEL, standing declaration that the employer qualifies under ET art. 114-1. T2R2-076 is a PER-EMPLOYEE, PER-MONTH application of it, and may be S only where A1-033 is S. The employee test has two parts: (a) the TOTAL EARNINGS of the month (total devengado) - the totality of what the worker receives in the month, every payment of the period whatever its salary character (Decreto 1828 de 2013 art. 7) - must be BELOW 10 SMMLV = COP 17,509,050 in 2026 (ET art. 114-1) - an earnings test, not an IBC test; and (b) in addition, where the health base (T2R2-043) is at or above 10 SMMLV the field MUST be N (AT2 v30 campo 76). Where a NATURAL-person employer employs two or more workers and the base reaches 10 SMMLV it must also be N. Where T2R2-076 is S, set T2R2-054 to the EMPLOYEE rate alone (4.0000%), and set the SENA and ICBF rates and values (T2R2-066 to T2R2-069) to zero.
Population Rules!D7	Because the test is MONTHLY, an employee who crosses 10 SMMLV in a bonus month loses the exoneration for that month alone and regains it the next. Evaluating it annually, or at hire, systematically under-contributes. The same test drives Form 350 casilla 27.
Population Rules!A8	═══ CRITICAL VALIDATION RULES ═══
Population Rules!A9	Header-to-detail consistency
Population Rules!B9	Engine (validation)
Population Rules!C9	Before submission assert: T2R1-004, 005, 006 and 007 equal File Tipo 1 campos 1, 2, 3 and 4 byte for byte; T2R1-011, 012 and 013 equal File Tipo 1 campos 10, 5 and 6; T2R1-021 equals File Tipo 1 campo 30; T2R1-019 equals the count of UNIQUE contributors in the detail EXCLUDING those with contributor type 40; and T2R1-020 equals the sum of T2R2-045 (IBC CCF, campo 45) across the detail.
Population Rules!D9	The type-40 exclusion from the contributor count is easy to miss and produces a header-to-detail mismatch the operador rejects outright.
Population Rules!A10	Pension rate forced to zero
Population Rules!B10	Engine (validation)
Population Rules!C10	Where the contributor sub-type (T2R2-006) is 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9 or 12, the pension rate (T2R2-046) MUST be 0 and no pension contribution may be settled.
Population Rules!D10	The electronic payroll equivalent is narrower - only SubTipoTrabajador 01 forces the rate to zero there. Map between the two lists; do not assume they agree.
Population Rules!A11	Health rate may be zero only in two cases
Population Rules!B11	Engine (validation)
Population Rules!C11	The annex is explicit: T2R2-054 may be reported as zero ONLY where (a) SLN (T2R2-024) is X and the exoneration flag (T2R2-076) is S, or (b) SLN is C and the employer's legal nature is public. And where the health contribution (T2R2-055) is zero, the health days (T2R2-037), the health base (T2R2-043) and the health rate (T2R2-054) must ALL be zero - except for those same SLN-plus-exoneration contributors.
Population Rules!D11	This is a four-field interlock. Implement it as one validation, not four independent ones.
Population Rules!A12	Change event flag to date-pair interlock
Population Rules!B12	Engine (validation)
Population Rules!C12	Every marked change event flag must have its corresponding date field or pair populated, and every populated date field must have its flag marked: ING to 080, RET to 081, VSP to 082, SLN to 083 and 084, IGE to 085 and 086, LMA to 087 and 088, VAC-LR to 089 and 090, VCT to 091 and 092, IRL days to 093 and 094, TAE to 034, TAP to 032, AVP to 048 and 049.
Population Rules!D12	Build the interlock as a table, not as a chain of conditionals - there are twelve pairs and a missed one produces a rejection at the operador rather than at Mercans.
Population Rules!A13	Contribution calculation checks published in the annex
Population Rules!B13	Engine (validation)
Population Rules!C13	The annex prints the arithmetic the operador will apply: campo 55 = campo 43 x campo 54; campo 65 = campo 45 x campo 64; campo 67 = campo 95 x campo 66; campo 69 = campo 95 x campo 68; campo 71 = campo 95 x campo 70; campo 73 = campo 95 x campo 72. Note that the CCF uses the CCF base while SENA, ICBF, ESAP and MEN all use the PARAFISCAL base.
Population Rules!D13	Reproduce these checks internally before submission. Each must then be rounded UP to the next multiple of 100.
Population Rules!A14	═══ AMOUNT FORMAT RULES ═══
Population Rules!A15	Rounding is UP, not to nearest
Population Rules!B15	Engine (formatting)
Population Rules!C15	Round the contribution base UP to the whole peso, and round EACH contribution value UP to the next multiple of 100 (Decree 1990 of 2016 art. 1). Worked illustration: occupational risk at class I on a base of 12,000,000 is 0.522% = 62,640, which becomes 62,700.
Population Rules!D15	This is neither the electronic payroll convention (2 decimals, round-half-to-even) nor the Form 350 convention (nearest 1,000). Blending them produces reconciliation differences that look like errors but are not - the reconciliation must be run on the UNROUNDED values with a tolerance.
Population Rules!A16	Numeric and alphanumeric justification
Population Rules!B16	Engine (formatting)
Population Rules!C16	Numeric fields are right-justified and ZERO-filled to the left; alphanumeric fields are left-justified and SPACE-filled to the right. The annex states this at s.0 and it applies without exception across all three records.
Population Rules!D16	An empty numeric field is a run of zeros, not a run of spaces. This catches optional numeric fields such as A1-016 (Fax) and the unused parafiscal rate and value fields.
Population Rules!A17	Rate fields carry implied decimals
Population Rules!B17	Engine (formatting)
Population Rules!C17	The rate fields are numeric with implied decimals in their published width - seven positions for T2R2-046, 054, 064, 066, 068, 070 and 072, and nine for T2R2-061. The implied decimal placement of each rate field is held in the file-format configuration as published by the chosen information operator; the annex does not print it unambiguously for every rate field.
Population Rules!D17	This is the one presentation detail of the layout that the annex leaves under-specified, and it differs between the seven-position and nine-position rate fields.
Population Rules!A18	═══ PAYROLL-SPECIFIC POPULATION RULES ═══
Population Rules!A19	The 40% non-salary clamp
Population Rules!B19	Payroll engine
Population Rules!C19	Total remuneration for the period = salary payments + payments agreed as non-salary under an express CST art. 128 pact + paid leave; indemnities, unpaid leave, incapacities and social benefits are left out (UGPP, ABC Empleadores 2025). Where the pacted non-salary payments exceed 40% of total remuneration, add the EXCESS back into the three contribution bases for pension, health and occupational risk (Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30; Consejo de Estado, Sección Cuarta, sentencia de unificación exp. 25185 of 9 December 2021, rule 3); the CCF and parafiscal bases of Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17 do not take it. Payments that are non-salary by nature or by law (transport allowance, occasional per diem, means and tools to do the job, mere liberality) stay out of both sides of the test (Consejo de Estado, Sección Cuarta, sentencia de unificación exp. 25185 of 9 December 2021, rule 3; UGPP, ABC Empleadores 2025). Store the added-back amount as its own quantity: it is IN the contribution base but OUT of the statutory-benefit base for cesantias, prima and the severance indemnity.
Population Rules!D19	The clamp is computed on the period's totals, so a single large non-salary bonus can push an otherwise compliant employee over the line for one month. It also interacts with the exoneration test, because the added-back amount raises the health base toward the 10-SMMLV threshold.
Population Rules!A20	All-inclusive salary at 70%
Population Rules!B20	Payroll engine
Population Rules!C20	Where the employee is on all-inclusive salary (CST art. 132 num. 2, minimum 13 SMMLV = COP 22,761,765 in 2026), mark T2R2-041 and set the four IBC fields to 70% of the integral salary. The remaining 30% is the statutory prestacional factor and is not a contribution base.
Population Rules!D20	Note the interaction with the ceiling: 70% of a large integral salary can still exceed 25 SMMLV, in which case the ceiling applies to the 70% figure, not to the full salary - for the pension, health and occupational-risk IBC only; the CCF base (T2R2-045) and the parafiscal base (T2R2-095) take the 70% figure without a ceiling.
Population Rules!A21	Work centre and risk class
Population Rules!B21	Employee master data
Population Rules!C21	Carry the work centre on the employee record and derive the risk class from it, not from the employer. Where an employee performs several occupations, contribute at the HIGHEST class (Decree 1072 of 2015 art. 2.2.4.2.5.6). Where the employee moves work centre mid-period, mark VCT (T2R2-029) and populate T2R2-091 and T2R2-092.
Population Rules!D21	A mid-period move can change the ARL rate; the annex does not prescribe a split of the period between the two work centres.
Population Rules!A22	Foreign employee electing out of pension
Population Rules!B22	Employee master data
Population Rules!C22	Mark T2R2-007 where the employee is a foreign national who has proved affiliation to a pension system abroad and elected out under Decree 1072 of 2015 art. 2.2.1.2.1.4. Health, occupational risk and the parafiscal levies remain fully payable. The election narrows the valid document types to CE, PA, CD, SC and PE.
Population Rules!D22	THIS IS NOT A TAX FIELD. It says nothing about tax residence: the same employee may be withheld at the flat 20% of ET art. 408 on sheet 2 of Form 350 while contributing to Colombian health and occupational risk on ordinary terms.
Population Rules!A23	Incapacity and leave advanced by the employer
Population Rules!B23	Payroll engine
Population Rules!C23	Colombian employers universally ADVANCE the EPS and ARL benefits through payroll and recover from the institution. Model the benefit as PAID to the employee and RECEIVABLE from the institution, but do NOT report it in the PILA: fields T2R2-057 to T2R2-060 (IGE and LMA authorisation numbers and values) are always blank / zero under AT2 v30, so the IGE and LMA amounts and authorisation numbers are kept in payroll for the reimbursement claim only.
Population Rules!D23	Without the authorisation number the institution will not reimburse. The employer's first-two-days rate for general illness (Mercans default 66.67%) and the one-minimum-wage floor on incapacity pay are configurable client elections maintained in the payroll configuration; neither is reported in PILA.
Population Rules!A24	═══ CROSS-FIELD VALIDATIONS ═══
Population Rules!A25	A1-009 drives A1-004, A1-018, A1-020, A1-021 and A1-023
Population Rules!B25	Engine (validation)
Population Rules!C25	Where the employer person type is J, the verification digit and the legal representative's identification number, document type, first surname and first name are all mandatory. Where it is N, the verification digit must be ZERO and the legal-representative block is optional.
Population Rules!D25	Note the asymmetry: A1-019 (the representative's own verification digit) is ALWAYS zero whatever the person type, because the annex says so expressly.
Population Rules!A26	A1-010 drives A1-005 and A1-006
Population Rules!B26	Engine (validation)
Population Rules!C26	Where the form of presentation is U, the branch code and name must be BLANK. Where it is S, both must be populated - and where the branch has no name, the annex directs that the CODE be repeated in the name field.
Population Rules!D26	The same interlock repeats at T2R1-011, T2R1-012 and T2R1-013.
Population Rules!A27	T2R1-008 drives T2R1-009 and T2R1-010
Population Rules!B27	Engine (validation)
Population Rules!C27	Where the return type is E, A, I, M, S, Y, T, X or K, both associated-return fields must be blank (or zero for the numeric one). Where it is N or F they carry the period return; where it is U, the judicial deposit title; where it is J or O, the return of the original payment.
Population Rules!D27	The annex adds that where the operador cannot find the initial return for that contributor and period, it must NOT permit the correction return at all.
Population Rules!A28	A1-007 = D drives A1-031, A1-032 and A1-034
Population Rules!B28	Engine (validation)
Population Rules!C28	A Ley 1429 de 2010 beneficiary must carry the commercial-registry date and the registered department, and the registry date must fall between 29 December 2010 and 31 December 2014.
Population Rules!D28	The window is closed and historical: an employer registered after 2014 cannot be class D, whatever its size.
Population Rules!A29	T2R2-008 drives T2R2-009, T2R2-010 and T2R2-097
Population Rules!B29	Engine (validation)
Population Rules!C29	Where 'Colombiano en el exterior' is marked, the DIVIPOLA work-location department and municipality must be BLANK and the foreign filing date becomes relevant. The flag is available only for document types CC and TI.
Population Rules!D29	Contributor type 40 also blanks the two DIVIPOLA fields, for a different reason.
Population Rules!A30	Cross-report reconciliation
Population Rules!B30	Engine (reconciliation)
Population Rules!C30	After settlement assert: T2R2-055 (employee portion) reconciles to electronic payroll NIE163; the employee 4% within T2R2-047 reconciles to NIE166; T2R2-051 and T2R2-052 reconcile to NIE168 and NIE170. Reconcile on the UNROUNDED values with a tolerance of 100 pesos per contribution, because PILA rounds up to the next 100 and the XML does not.
Population Rules!D30	The employer-borne amounts - pension 12%, health 8.5%, occupational risk, CCF, SENA, ICBF - have NO electronic payroll counterpart at all and cannot be reconciled to it. They reconcile only to the employer-cost ledger.

Example Analysis!A12	S
Example Analysis!B12	A1-033 - Contributor exempt
Example Analysis!C12	The employer declares the ET art. 114-1 exoneration.
Example Analysis!D12	This is the standing employer-level declaration. It is applied per employee per month at T2R2-076, and it rests on the same test as Form 350 casilla 27.
Example Analysis!A13	N

Example Analysis!A14	═══ ARCHIVO TIPO 2, REGISTRO TIPO 1 - KEY FIELDS ═══

Example Analysis!C15	Header record, electronic modality, employee return.
Example Analysis!D15	Record type 01 distinguishes the header from the 02 detail records in the same file.
Example Analysis!A16	2026-03 / 2026-04
Example Analysis!B16	T2R1-015 / T2R1-016
Example Analysis!C16	Pension, occupational risk and levies for March; HEALTH FOR APRIL.
Example Analysis!D16	COLOMBIA'S MOST-MISSED PILA RULE. Health is paid one month in advance. Setting these equal is the classic first-run defect. Note also that T2R1-015 is calculated by the OPERADOR while T2R1-016 is supplied by the EMPLOYER.
Example Analysis!A17	00001
Example Analysis!B17	T2R1-019 - Numero total de cotizantes
Example Analysis!C17	One contributor.

Example Analysis!A18	000012000000
Example Analysis!B18	T2R1-020 - Value total de la nomina
Example Analysis!C18	The sum of the CCF base (campo 45, T2R2-045) across the detail for every contributor type other than independents - not the parafiscal base in campo 95 (T2R2-095).
Example Analysis!D18	AT2 v30 §2.1.1.1 campo 20 defines this header value as the sum of campo 45 'IBC CCF' of Record Tipo 2 across the detail; it is ZERO where no contributor type in the return is obliged to contribute to a family compensation fund. Do not derive it from the parafiscal base in campo 95. In this worked example the two bases coincide (COP 12,000,000), so the raw value is unchanged.
Example Analysis!A19	═══ ARCHIVO TIPO 2, REGISTRO TIPO 2 - BASES AND DAYS ═══
Example Analysis!A20	30 / 30 / 30 / 30
Example Analysis!B20	T2R2-036 to T2R2-039
Example Analysis!C20	Full contributory month for pension, health, occupational risk and the CCF.
Example Analysis!D20	Four SEPARATE counters. They diverge as soon as a change event enters - a five-day SLN gives 25 and a date pair spanning exactly five days.
Example Analysis!A21	012000000 x4
Example Analysis!B21	T2R2-042 to T2R2-045
Example Analysis!C21	The four IBC values, all COP 12,000,000 in this simple month.
Example Analysis!D21	T2R2-042, T2R2-043 and T2R2-044 (pension, health and ARL): between the floor of 1 SMMLV (COP 1,750,905) and the ceiling of 25 SMMLV (COP 43,772,625). T2R2-045 (IBC CCF): no 25 SMMLV ceiling - the base is the monthly salary payroll (Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17), including paid rest days, 70% for an integral salary (Ley 789 de 2002 art. 49); only the floor applies. The four values coincide here only because there is no non-salary payment, no change event and the salary is below 25 SMMLV.
Example Analysis!A22	012000000
Example Analysis!B22	T2R2-095 - IBC otros parafiscales
Example Analysis!C22	The FIFTH base, for SENA, ICBF, ESAP and MEN.
Example Analysis!D22	Defined by Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17: includes paid rest days and is UNCAPPED. Equal to the others here, but it diverges as soon as the employee earns above the ceiling.
Example Analysis!A23	═══ REGISTRO TIPO 2 - PENSION ═══
Example Analysis!A24	0160000
Example Analysis!B24	T2R2-046 - Pension contribution rate
Example Analysis!C24	16% total - employer 12% plus employee 4%.
Example Analysis!D24	Would be forced to 0 if the contributor sub-type were 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9 or 12.
Example Analysis!A25	001920000
Example Analysis!B25	T2R2-047 - Compulsory pension contribution
Example Analysis!C25	16% of COP 12,000,000 = COP 1,920,000. Already a multiple of 100.
Example Analysis!D25	THE WHOLE contribution, employer and employee together. The electronic payroll reports only the employee's 4% (COP 480,000) at NIE166 - the two figures are not interchangeable.
Example Analysis!A26	000060000
Example Analysis!B26	T2R2-051 - FSP subcuenta de solidaridad
Example Analysis!C26	0.5% of COP 12,000,000 = COP 60,000 (50% of the additional 1%), because the base is at or above 4 SMMLV.
Example Analysis!D26	Employee-borne and ADDITIONAL to the 4% pension. Reconciles to electronic payroll NIE168. Reported SEPARATELY from pension in both artefacts - merging them is the most common Colombian mapping error.
Example Analysis!A27	000060000
Example Analysis!B27	T2R2-052 - FSP subcuenta de subsistencia
Example Analysis!C27	0.5% of COP 12,000,000 = COP 60,000 (the other 50% of the additional 1%); no band surcharge because the base is below 16 SMMLV.
Example Analysis!D27	Both sub-accounts start at 4 SMMLV; only the subsistence one rises by band from 16 SMMLV (Ley 100 art. 27 as amended by Ley 797 art. 8).
Example Analysis!A28	═══ REGISTRO TIPO 2 - HEALTH AND THE EXONERATION ═══
Example Analysis!A29	S
Example Analysis!B29	T2R2-076 - Cotizante exonerado de pago de aporte salud, SENA e ICBF
Example Analysis!C29	The employee earns below 10 SMMLV and the employer has declared itself exonerated in A1-033.
Example Analysis!D29	TESTED MONTHLY. A bonus that pushes this employee above COP 17,509,050 in one month forces this field to N for that month alone.
Example Analysis!A30	0040000
Example Analysis!B30	T2R2-054 - Contribution rate salud
Example Analysis!C30	4% - THE EMPLOYEE RATE ALONE, because the exoneration applies.
Example Analysis!D30	The annex directs at s.2.1.2.3.21 that where A1-033 and T2R2-076 are both S, the rate to enter here is 'la tarifa correspondiente al trabajador'. Without the exoneration this field would read 0125000 (12.5%) and the contribution would be COP 1,500,000.
Example Analysis!A31	000480000
Example Analysis!B31	T2R2-055 - Compulsory contribution a salud
Example Analysis!C31	4% of COP 12,000,000 = COP 480,000. The annex's own check is campo 43 x campo 54.
Example Analysis!D31	Reconciles to electronic payroll NIE163, which reports the same COP 480,000 - here they coincide because the figure is already a multiple of 100.
Example Analysis!A32	═══ REGISTRO TIPO 2 - OCCUPATIONAL RISK AND LEVIES ═══
Example Analysis!A33	1 / 000005220
Example Analysis!B33	T2R2-078 / T2R2-061
Example Analysis!C33	Risk class I, rate 0.522%.
Example Analysis!D33	The class attaches to the WORK CENTRE (T2R2-062), not to the employer.
Example Analysis!A34	000062700
Example Analysis!B34	T2R2-063 - Compulsory occupational-risk contribution
Example Analysis!C34	0.522% of COP 12,000,000 = COP 62,640, ROUNDED UP to the next multiple of 100 = COP 62,700.
Example Analysis!D34	THE CLEAREST ILLUSTRATION OF THE PILA ROUNDING CONVENTION. Decree 1990 of 2016 art. 1 rounds UP, never to nearest. Employer-borne in full - there is no employee occupational-risk deduction in Colombia, which is why no electronic payroll element corresponds to it.
Example Analysis!A35	0040000 / 000480000
Example Analysis!B35	T2R2-064 / T2R2-065
Example Analysis!C35	CCF at 4% of COP 12,000,000 = COP 480,000.
Example Analysis!D35	NEVER exonerated under art. 114-1. Only the Ley 1429 art. 5 regime gives relief on the CCF, and this employer has declared N at A1-034.
Example Analysis!A36	0000000 / 000000000
Example Analysis!B36	T2R2-066 / T2R2-067
Example Analysis!C36	SENA at 0% and nil.
Example Analysis!D36	Exonerated under ET art. 114-1 because T2R2-076 is S. Without the exoneration this would be 2% of the PARAFISCAL base = COP 240,000.
Example Analysis!A37	0000000 / 000000000
Example Analysis!B37	T2R2-068 / T2R2-069
Example Analysis!C37	ICBF at 0% and nil.
Example Analysis!D37	Exonerated. Without it, 3% of the parafiscal base = COP 360,000. The exoneration is therefore worth COP 1,620,000 a month for this one employee - health 1,020,000 plus SENA 240,000 plus ICBF 360,000.
Example Analysis!A38	0 / 0
Example Analysis!B38	T2R2-070 to T2R2-073
Example Analysis!C38	ESAP and MEN at nil.
Example Analysis!D38	Public-sector levies. Out of scope for private corporate payroll, but the positions remain in the record and must be zero-filled.
Example Analysis!A39	═══ CROSS-REPORT RECONCILIATION FROM THIS PLANILLA ═══
Example Analysis!A40	480000
Example Analysis!B40	Equals Electronic payroll NIE163
Example Analysis!C40	Employee health.
Example Analysis!D40	Coincides exactly here. It will differ by up to 99 pesos where the raw figure is not a multiple of 100 - PILA rounds up, the XML does not.
Example Analysis!A41	480000 (4% within 1,920,000)
Example Analysis!B41	Equals Electronic payroll NIE166
Example Analysis!C41	Employee pension.
Example Analysis!D41	The XML reports the employee 4% alone; PILA campo 47 reports the whole 16%. Derive, do not copy.
Example Analysis!A42	120000
Example Analysis!B42	Equals Electronic payroll NIE168
Example Analysis!C42	Solidarity fund.
Example Analysis!D42	Reported separately in both artefacts. Never merged into pension.
Example Analysis!A43	(no counterpart)
Example Analysis!B43	Employer 12% pension, 8.5% health, ARL, CCF, SENA, ICBF
Example Analysis!C43	COP 1,440,000 + 0 + 62,700 + 480,000 + 0 + 0 = COP 1,982,700 of employer cost.
Example Analysis!D43	None of this appears on the electronic payroll, which reports only what the employee accrued and what was deducted from the employee. It reconciles to the employer-cost ledger alone.
Sample File!A1	SAMPLE FILE - FIXED-WIDTH RECORDS GENERATED FROM THE OFFICIAL POSITION TABLE
Sample File!A3	PROVENANCE NOTE - READ FIRST
Sample File!A4	═══════════
Sample File!A5	The three records below were GENERATED PROGRAMMATICALLY by placing each
Sample File!A6	value at the position published in the official Technical Annex 2 (Resolution
Sample File!A7	MSPS 2388 de 2016, AT2 version 30, 24-07-2026) and applying the annex's own
Sample File!A8	justification rules - numeric fields right-justified and zero-filled,
Sample File!A9	alphanumeric fields left-justified and space-filled.
Sample File!A11	The record lengths are therefore EXACT, not approximate:
Sample File!A12	    Archivo Tipo 1                   567 characters (annex total: 567)
Sample File!A13	    Archivo Tipo 2, Registro Tipo 1  359 characters (annex total: 359)
Sample File!A14	    Archivo Tipo 2, Registro Tipo 2  693 characters (annex total: 693)
Sample File!A16	This is NOT an official MSPS sample file - none is published - but the
Sample File!A17	LAYOUT is the official layout, byte for byte. What each operador de
Sample File!A18	information wraps around these two files, and what additional validations it
Sample File!A19	applies, is operador-specific and is held in the information-operator configuration.
Sample File!A21	Administradora codes (AFP001, EPS001, CCF001, ARL001) are illustrative valueS. The
Sample File!A22	real six-position codes are the Ministry catalogue codes distributed by the chosen operador.
Sample File!A24	Values are the worked example on the Example Analysis sheet: MERCANS
Sample File!A25	COLOMBIA S.A.S., NIT 900123456-8, one contributor on COP 12,000,000 for
Sample File!A26	March 2026, exonerated under ET art. 114-1, risk class I.

Sample File!A137	Registro Tipo 10  Total aportes a la ESAP
Sample File!A138	Registro Tipo 11  Total aportes al Ministerio de Educacion Nacional
Sample File!A139	Registro Tipo 12  Total a pagar durante el periodo
Sample File!A141	These records also carry the days-in-arrears count and the interest on
Sample File!A142	arrears, which the operador computes from the payment date. The employer
Sample File!A143	neither produces nor validates them - but the ENGINE MUST PARSE THEM from
Sample File!A144	the settlement response in order to reconcile, capture the return number
Sample File!A145	and payment date, and detect rejected contributor records.
